Option Explicit

' Paste this complete file into a standard module named Controller2Setup.
Public Sub InstallController2()
    Dim project As Object, component As Object, sheet As Worksheet
    Dim previousEvents As Boolean, index As Long, phase As String
    previousEvents = Application.EnableEvents
    On Error GoTo Failed
    phase = "導入前の確認"
    If ThisWorkbook.FileFormat <> xlOpenXMLWorkbookMacroEnabled Or Len(ThisWorkbook.Path) = 0 Then _
        Err.Raise 5, , "保存済みの .xlsm の作業コピーを使用してください。"
    If ThisWorkbook.ReadOnly Then Err.Raise 5, , "編集可能な作業コピーを使用してください。"
    If VBA.UserForms.Count > 0 Then Err.Raise 5, , "操作画面とカレンダーを閉じてから実行してください。"
    If ThisWorkbook.Sheets.Count <> 3 Then Err.Raise 5, , "設定シート3枚のコントローラーを使用してください。"
    For index = 1 To 3
        Set sheet = ThisWorkbook.Worksheets(Choose(index, "Address", "Fields", "Operations"))
    Next index
    Set project = ThisWorkbook.VBProject
    If project.Protection <> 0 Then Err.Raise 5, , "VBAプロジェクトがロックされています。"
    If project.VBComponents("Controller2Setup").Type <> 1 Then Err.Raise 5, , "貼付先は Controller2Setup 標準モジュールです。"

    Application.EnableEvents = False
    phase = "旧VBAコンポーネントの取外し"
    For index = project.VBComponents.Count To 1 Step -1
        Set component = project.VBComponents(index)
        If component.Type <> 100 And component.Name <> "Controller2Setup" Then project.VBComponents.Remove component
    Next index
    phase = "VBAコンポーネントの再構築"
    Set component = project.VBComponents.Add(1)
    component.Name = "LedgerTransfer"
    Set component = project.VBComponents.Add(1)
    component.Name = "LedgerTransferFlows"
    Set component = project.VBComponents.Add(1)
    component.Name = "OperationAudit"
    Set component = project.VBComponents.Add(1)
    component.Name = "DailyBackup"
    Set component = project.VBComponents.Add(3)
    component.Name = "LedgerControllerForm"
    DesignLedgerControllerForm component
    Set component = project.VBComponents.Add(1)
    component.Name = "ControllerData"
    Set component = project.VBComponents.Add(1)
    component.Name = "ControllerActions"
    Set component = project.VBComponents.Add(2)
    component.Name = "ExtendedListBox"
    Set component = project.VBComponents.Add(2)
    component.Name = "ExtendedListBoxLabel"
    Set component = project.VBComponents.Add(2)
    component.Name = "CCalendarDayButton"
    Set component = project.VBComponents.Add(3)
    component.Name = "frmCalendar"
    DesignfrmCalendar component
    Set component = project.VBComponents.Add(1)
    component.Name = "modCalendar"
    Set component = project.VBComponents.Add(1)
    component.Name = "modUITheme"
    Set component = project.VBComponents.Add(2)
    component.Name = "ControllerDateButton"
    Set component = project.VBComponents.Add(1)
    component.Name = "CollectionPaging"
    Set component = project.VBComponents.Add(2)
    component.Name = "LedgerReadBatch"
    phase = "Insert code"
    WriteCode project.VBComponents("ThisWorkbook"), SourceThisWorkbook()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents(ThisWorkbook.Worksheets("Address").CodeName), SourceSheet1()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents(ThisWorkbook.Worksheets("Fields").CodeName), SourceSheet2()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents("LedgerTransfer"), SourceLedgerTransfer()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents("LedgerTransferFlows"), SourceLedgerTransferFlows()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents("OperationAudit"), SourceOperationAudit()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents("DailyBackup"), SourceDailyBackup()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents(ThisWorkbook.Worksheets("Operations").CodeName), SourceSheet3()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents("LedgerControllerForm"), SourceLedgerControllerForm()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents("ControllerData"), SourceControllerData()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents("ControllerActions"), SourceControllerActions()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents("ExtendedListBox"), SourceExtendedListBox()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents("ExtendedListBoxLabel"), SourceExtendedListBoxLabel()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents("CCalendarDayButton"), SourceCCalendarDayButton()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents("frmCalendar"), SourcefrmCalendar()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents("modCalendar"), SourcemodCalendar()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents("modUITheme"), SourcemodUITheme()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents("ControllerDateButton"), SourceControllerDateButton()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents("CollectionPaging"), SourceCollectionPaging()
    WriteCode project.VBComponents("LedgerReadBatch"), SourceLedgerReadBatch()
    Application.EnableEvents = previousEvents
    Application.StatusBar = "Controller2 install complete"
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    Application.EnableEvents = previousEvents
    MsgBox phase & vbCrLf & Err.Description & vbCrLf & "この作業コピーを保存せずに閉じてください。", vbExclamation
End Sub

Private Sub WriteCode(ByVal component As Object, ByVal source As String)
    Dim code As Object
    Set code = component.CodeModule
    If code.CountOfLines > 0 Then code.DeleteLines 1, code.CountOfLines
    code.InsertLines 1, source
End Sub

Private Function SourceThisWorkbook() As String
    SourceThisWorkbook = SourceThisWorkbook & PartThisWorkbook1()
End Function
Private Function PartThisWorkbook1() As String
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "Private Sub Workbook_Open()" & vbCrLf
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "    Dim batch As LedgerReadBatch" & vbCrLf
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "    If Application.Visible Then Set batch = New LedgerReadBatch" & vbCrLf
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "    RunConfiguredDailyBackup batch" & vbCrLf
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "    If Application.Visible Then OpenLedgerControllerWithBatch batch" & vbCrLf
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "Private Sub Workbook_BeforeClose(Cancel As Boolean)" & vbCrLf
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "    On Error GoTo Failed" & vbCrLf
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "    If Cancel Then Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "    Cancel = Not CloseLedgerController()" & vbCrLf
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "Failed:" & vbCrLf
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "    Cancel = True" & vbCrLf
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "    MsgBox ""終了処理を完了できませんでした。"" & vbCrLf & Err.Description, vbExclamation, ""管理簿コントローラー""" & vbCrLf
    PartThisWorkbook1 = PartThisWorkbook1 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
End Function

    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "' Values are raw Value2 values, not formatted display strings." & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Set NewLedgerRecord = record" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "Public Function ReadLedgerRecord(ByVal fileId As String, ByVal identity As Object) As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Dim settings As Object, fields As Object, definition As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Dim book As Workbook, sheet As Worksheet, rowNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Dim errorNumber As Long, errorMessage As String" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    On Error GoTo Failed" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Set fields = LoadLedgerFields()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Set settings = LoadLedgerAddresses()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Set definition = settings(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Set book = OpenLedger(definition, True)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Set sheet = book.Worksheets(definition(""Sheet""))" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    ValidateHeaders sheet, definition, fields" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    rowNumber = FindRecordRow(sheet, definition, fields, identity)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    If rowNumber = 0 Then RaiseEngine ""Record not found: "" & fileId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Set ReadLedgerRecord = ReadRecord(sheet, rowNumber, fields)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    book.Close SaveChanges:=False" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Exit Function" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    If Not book Is Nothing Then book.Close SaveChanges:=False" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Err.Raise errorNumber, ""ReadLedgerRecord"", errorMessage" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "' journalAddress: dedicated, pre-created empty .xlsx, one worksheet, no other data." & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "' Exclusive access must be arranged externally; this flag is NOT a lock." & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "Public Sub ExecuteLedgerTransfer(ByVal instructions As Collection, ByVal journalAddress As String, ByVal hasExclusiveAccess As Boolean, Optional ByVal operationKind As String)" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Dim fields As Object, Addresses As Object, plans As Collection, groups As Object" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Dim fileId As Variant, journal As Workbook, journalSheet As Worksheet" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Dim oldEvents As Boolean, oldScreen As Boolean, oldSecurity As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Dim errorNumber As Long, errorMessage As String, currentFile As String" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Dim hasJournal As Boolean, hasStarted As Boolean, originalPlans As Collection" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    Dim auditActor As String, auditOperation As String, auditEntries As Collection, summary As Collection" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer1 = PartLedgerTransfer1 & "    auditActor = OperationActor()" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartLedgerTransfer2() As String
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    If isResume Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        auditOperation = ""Recovery""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        auditOperation = PlannedOperation(instructions, operationKind)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    If isRunning Or Not hasExclusiveAccess Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        If isRunning Then errorMessage = ""Transfer is already running."" Else errorMessage = ""Exclusive access must be arranged before transfer.""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        AppendOperationAudit auditActor, auditOperation, journalAddress, ""ERROR"", errorMessage, summary" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        RaiseEngine errorMessage" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    isRunning = True" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    oldEvents = Application.EnableEvents" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    oldScreen = Application.ScreenUpdating" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    oldSecurity = Application.AutomationSecurity" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    On Error GoTo Failed" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    Application.EnableEvents = False" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    Application.ScreenUpdating = False" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    Application.AutomationSecurity = 3" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    Set fields = LoadLedgerFields()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    Set Addresses = LoadLedgerAddresses()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    Set books = NewLedgerRecord()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    Set journal = OpenDedicatedBook(journalAddress, False)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    If journal.Worksheets.count <> 1 Then RaiseEngine ""Journal must have exactly one worksheet.""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    Set journalSheet = journal.Worksheets(1)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    If isResume Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        If Left$(CStr(journalSheet.range(""I1"").Value2), 1) = ""S"" Then _" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "            auditOperation = ""Recovery/"" & Mid$(CStr(journalSheet.range(""I1"").Value2), 2)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        Set plans = ReadJournal(journalSheet, fields, Addresses)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        hasJournal = True" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        If Application.CountA(journalSheet.UsedRange) <> 0 Then RaiseEngine ""Journal is not empty. Use Resume for an existing transaction.""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        Set plans = BuildPlans(instructions, fields, Addresses)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    Set auditEntries = AuditChanges(plans, fields, Addresses)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    AppendOperationAudit auditActor, auditOperation, journalAddress, ""START"", vbNullString, auditEntries" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    Set groups = GroupPlans(plans)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    ' Open and validate all destinations before the first write." & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    For Each fileId In groups.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        Set definition = Addresses(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        Set book = OpenLedger(definition, False)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        books.Add CStr(fileId), book" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        Set group = groups(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        CheckGroup book, definition, fields, group, isResume" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    Next fileId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    If Not isResume Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        WriteJournal journalSheet, plans, fields, Addresses" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        journalSheet.range(""I1"").Value2 = ""S"" & auditOperation" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        journal.Save" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        journal.Close SaveChanges:=False" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        Set journal = Nothing" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        Set journal = OpenDedicatedBook(journalAddress, False)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        Set journalSheet = journal.Worksheets(1)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        Set originalPlans = plans" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        Set plans = ReadJournal(journalSheet, fields, Addresses)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        ComparePlans originalPlans, plans, fields" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        Set groups = GroupPlans(plans)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        hasJournal = True" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    SetJournalStatus journal, ""PENDING"", """"" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "    For Each fileId In groups.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        currentFile = CStr(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        Set definition = Addresses(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        Set group = groups(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        Set book = books(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        CheckGroup book, definition, fields, group, isResume" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        hasStarted = True" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        ApplyGroup book, definition, fields, group, isResume" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        If Not book.saved Then book.Save" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        book.Close SaveChanges:=False" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer2 = PartLedgerTransfer2 & "        books.Remove fileId" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartLedgerTransfer3() As String
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        Set book = OpenLedger(definition, True)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        books.Add CStr(fileId), book" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        VerifyGroup book, definition, fields, group" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        book.Close SaveChanges:=False" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        books.Remove fileId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        SetJournalStatus journal, ""PENDING"", ""Verified: "" & currentFile" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    Next fileId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    SetJournalStatus journal, ""COMPLETE"", ""All destinations reopened and verified.""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    GoTo Cleanup" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "Failed:" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    errorNumber = Err.number: errorMessage = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    If hasJournal And Not journal Is Nothing Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        SetJournalStatus journal, ""PENDING"", currentFile & "": "" & errorMessage" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "Cleanup:" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    If Not books Is Nothing Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        For Each fileId In books.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "            Set book = books(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "            book.Close SaveChanges:=False" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        Next fileId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    If Not journal Is Nothing Then journal.Close SaveChanges:=False" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    Application.AutomationSecurity = oldSecurity" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    Application.ScreenUpdating = oldScreen" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    Application.EnableEvents = oldEvents" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    isRunning = False" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    If errorNumber <> 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        If hasStarted Or hasJournal Then errorMessage = errorMessage & "" | Inspect journal and resume: "" & journalAddress" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        AppendOperationAudit auditActor, auditOperation, journalAddress, ""ERROR"", errorMessage, summary" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        Err.Raise errorNumber, ""LedgerTransfer"", errorMessage" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    AppendOperationAudit auditActor, auditOperation, journalAddress, ""COMPLETE"", vbNullString, summary" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "Public Function LoadLedgerFields() As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    Dim sheet As Worksheet, fields As Object, columns As Object, ranks As Object, definition As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    Dim rowNumber As Long, lastRow As Long, fieldId As String, columnName As String, columnNumber As Long, rank As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    Set sheet = ThisWorkbook.Worksheets(""Fields"")" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    Set fields = NewLedgerRecord(): Set columns = NewLedgerRecord(): Set ranks = NewLedgerRecord()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    lastRow = sheet.Cells(sheet.rows.count, 5).End(xlUp).row" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "    For rowNumber = 2 To lastRow" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        fieldId = Trim$(CStr(sheet.Cells(rowNumber, 5).Value2))" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        If Len(fieldId) = 0 Then RaiseEngine ""Missing field ID at Fields row "" & rowNumber" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        columnName = UCase$(Trim$(CStr(sheet.Cells(rowNumber, 2).Value2)))" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        If Len(columnName) = 0 Or Len(columnName) > 3 Or columnName Like ""*[!A-Z]*"" Then RaiseEngine ""Invalid field column: "" & columnName" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        columnNumber = sheet.range(columnName & ""1"").column" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        If fields.Exists(fieldId) Or columns.Exists(CStr(columnNumber)) Then RaiseEngine ""Duplicate field ID or column: "" & fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        Set definition = NewLedgerRecord()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        definition.Add ""Header"", CStr(sheet.Cells(rowNumber, 1).Value2)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer3 = PartLedgerTransfer3 & "        If Len(definition(""Header"")) = 0 Then RaiseEngine ""Missing field header: "" & fieldId" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer10 = PartLedgerTransfer10 & "    If IsEmpty(value) Then" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer11 = PartLedgerTransfer11 & "    rowNumber = 1" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer11 = PartLedgerTransfer11 & "    Do While rowNumber <= lastRow" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer11 = PartLedgerTransfer11 & "        planNumber = planNumber + 1" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer12 = PartLedgerTransfer12 & "        Set changes = NewLedgerRecord()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer12 = PartLedgerTransfer12 & "        For Each fieldId In fields.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer12 = PartLedgerTransfer12 & "            If plan(""Insert"") Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer12 = PartLedgerTransfer12 & "                If Not IsEmpty(afterRecord(fieldId)) Then changes.Add CStr(fieldId), afterRecord(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer12 = PartLedgerTransfer12 & "            ElseIf Not LedgerValuesEqual(beforeRecord(fieldId), afterRecord(fieldId)) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer12 = PartLedgerTransfer12 & "                changes.Add CStr(fieldId), afterRecord(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer12 = PartLedgerTransfer12 & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer12 = PartLedgerTransfer12 & "        Next fieldId" & vbCrLf
End Function

    PartLedgerTransfer13 = PartLedgerTransfer13 & "    If lastRow >= firstRow Then data = sheet.range(sheet.Cells(firstRow, 1), sheet.Cells(lastRow, maxColumn)).Value2" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartLedgerTransfer14() As String
    PartLedgerTransfer14 = PartLedgerTransfer14 & "    For Each key In index.keys" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer14 = PartLedgerTransfer14 & "                Else" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer14 = PartLedgerTransfer14 & "' Weekly source headers are explicitly mapped in Fields K; duplicate header names are rejected." & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer14 = PartLedgerTransfer14 & "            rowNumber = sheet.Cells(sheet.rows.count, columnNumber).End(xlUp).row" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartLedgerTransfer15() As String
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            If rowNumber > lastRow Then lastRow = rowNumber" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    Next fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    For rowNumber = firstRow To lastRow" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "        Set record = NewLedgerRecord(): hasData = False" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "        For Each fieldId In mapping.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            value = sheet.Cells(rowNumber, mapping(fieldId)).Value2" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            If Not IsEmpty(value) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "                If EncodeValue(value) <> ""S"" Then hasData = True" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            If EncodeValue(value) = ""S"" Then value = Empty" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            record.Add CStr(fieldId), value" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "        Next fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "        If hasData Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            For Each fieldId In requiredFields.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "                value = record(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "                If IsError(value) Or IsEmpty(value) Then _" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "                    RaiseEngine ""Missing or invalid required weekly value: "" & CStr(fieldId) & "" / row "" & rowNumber" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "                If Len(Trim$(CStr(value))) = 0 Then _" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "                    RaiseEngine ""Missing required weekly value: "" & CStr(fieldId) & "" / row "" & rowNumber" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            Next fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            key = LedgerRecordKey(record, fields)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            If seen.Exists(key) Then RaiseEngine ""Duplicate weekly source record.""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            seen.Add key, True" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            Records.Add record" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    Next rowNumber" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    book.Close SaveChanges:=False" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    Set ReadWeeklySourceRecords = Records" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "Failed:" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    errorNumber = Err.number: errorMessage = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    If Not book Is Nothing Then book.Close SaveChanges:=False" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    Err.Raise errorNumber, ""ReadWeeklySourceRecords"", errorMessage" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "Private Function WeeklySourceColumn(ByVal sheet As Worksheet, ByVal headerRow As Long, ByVal headers As Object, _" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "                                    ByVal headerName As String, ByVal columnSpec As String) As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    Dim columnNumber As Long, characterIndex As Long, actualHeader As String" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    If Len(columnSpec) = 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "        If headers.Exists(headerName) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            columnNumber = headers(headerName)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            If columnNumber < 1 Then RaiseEngine ""Ambiguous source header: "" & headerName" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "        If columnSpec Like ""*[!0-9]*"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            If Len(columnSpec) > 3 Or columnSpec Like ""*[!A-Z]*"" Then RaiseEngine ""Invalid weekly source column: "" & columnSpec" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            For characterIndex = 1 To Len(columnSpec)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "                columnNumber = columnNumber * 26 + Asc(Mid$(columnSpec, characterIndex, 1)) - Asc(""A"") + 1" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            Next characterIndex" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "        Else" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            columnNumber = PositiveInteger(columnSpec)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "        If columnNumber < 1 Or columnNumber > sheet.columns.count Then RaiseEngine ""Invalid weekly source column: "" & columnSpec" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "        actualHeader = CStr(sheet.Cells(headerRow, columnNumber).Value2)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "        If Len(actualHeader) = 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            columnNumber = 0" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "        ElseIf Len(headerName) > 0 And actualHeader <> headerName Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "            RaiseEngine ""Weekly source header mismatch at column "" & columnSpec & "": "" & headerName" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    WeeklySourceColumn = columnNumber" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "Private Sub ValidateDateStorage(ByVal previousValue As Variant, ByVal nextValue As Variant, ByVal fieldId As String)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    Dim previousIsCompact As Boolean, nextIsCompact As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "    If Left$(EncodeValue(previousValue), 1) = ""N"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer15 = PartLedgerTransfer15 & "        previousIsCompact = (CDbl(previousValue) >= 10000000# And CDbl(previousValue) <= 99999999#)" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartLedgerTransfer16() As String
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "        nextIsCompact = (CDbl(nextValue) >= 10000000# And CDbl(nextValue) <= 99999999#)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    ElseIf VarType(previousValue) = vbString Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "        previousIsCompact = (Len(CStr(previousValue)) = 8 And Not (CStr(previousValue) Like ""*[!0-9]*""))" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "        nextIsCompact = (Len(CStr(nextValue)) = 8 And Not (CStr(nextValue) Like ""*[!0-9]*""))" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    If previousIsCompact <> nextIsCompact Then RaiseEngine ""Date storage representation change: "" & fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "Public Sub RunConfiguredDailyBackup(Optional ByVal batch As LedgerReadBatch)" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    If Len(archive) = 0 Then Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    retention = config.range(""B5"").Value2" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    If batch Is Nothing Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "        Set Addresses = LoadLedgerAddresses()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "        Set Addresses = batch.Addresses" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    Set targets = NewLedgerRecord()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    For Each fileId In Addresses.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "        Set definition = Addresses(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "        targets.Add CStr(fileId), CStr(definition(""Address""))" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    Next fileId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    created = CreateDailyBackup(archive, targets, PositiveInteger(retention), observer:=batch)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "Failed:" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    Dim message As String" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    message = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    AppendOperationAudit OperationActor(), ""DailyBackup"", archive, ""ERROR"", message, entries" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    Debug.Print ""Daily backup: "" & message" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "Private Function PlannedOperation(ByVal instructions As Collection, ByVal explicitOperation As String) As String" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    Dim instruction As Object, operation As String" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    PlannedOperation = explicitOperation" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    If Len(explicitOperation) > 0 Then Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    PlannedOperation = ""LedgerTransfer""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    If instructions Is Nothing Then Exit Function" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "        If Not instruction.Exists(""Operation"") Then Exit Function" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "Private Function AuditChanges(ByVal plans As Collection, ByVal fields As Object, ByVal Addresses As Object) As Collection" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    Dim entries As New Collection, plan As Object, fieldId As Variant, beforeRecord As Object, afterRecord As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    Dim definition As Object, beforeValue As Variant, afterValue As Variant, key As String" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    For Each plan In plans" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "        Set definition = Addresses(plan(""FileId""))" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "        For Each fieldId In fields.keys" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "            If Not beforeRecord Is Nothing Then beforeValue = beforeRecord(fieldId)" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "        Next fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    Next plan" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "    Set AuditChanges = entries" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransfer16 = PartLedgerTransfer16 & "End Function" & vbCrLf

Private Function SourceLedgerTransferFlows() As String

Private Function PartLedgerTransferFlows1() As String

    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "' D / H / J flags are exact Japanese circle/cross values." & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "                                             title:=""Select the weekly source workbook"")" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "            If Not IsMissing(conflicts) Then Set conflicts = issues" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "            Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        sourcePath = CStr(report)" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "    Set Records = ReadWeeklySourceRecords(sourcePath)" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "    Set indexes = NewLedgerRecord()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "    masterId = MasterFileId(Addresses)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "    Set masterIndex = IndexWeeklyDestination(masterId, fields)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "    For Each fileId In Addresses.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        Set definition = Addresses(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        If definition(""Role"") = ""Branch"" Then indexes.Add fileId, IndexWeeklyDestination(CStr(fileId), fields)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "    Next fileId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "    For Each record In Records" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        key = LedgerRecordKey(record, fields)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        branchId = BranchFileId(Addresses, RequiredOffice(record))" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        misplaced = vbNullString" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        For Each fileId In indexes.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "            Set branchIndex = indexes(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "            If CStr(fileId) <> branchId And branchIndex.Exists(key) Then _" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "                misplaced = misplaced & ""Also exists in "" & CStr(fileId) & "". """ & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        Next fileId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        Set branchIndex = indexes(branchId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        details = vbNullString" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        If Not masterIndex.Exists(key) And Not branchIndex.Exists(key) And Len(misplaced) = 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "            pending.Add record" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        Else" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "            If masterIndex.Exists(key) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "                Set existing = masterIndex(key)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "                details = WeeklyDifferences(record, existing, masterId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "            Else" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "                details = ""Missing from "" & masterId & "". """ & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "            If branchIndex.Exists(key) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "                Set branchRecord = SelectFields(record, fields, ""Branch"")" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "                Set existing = branchIndex(key)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "                details = details & WeeklyDifferences(branchRecord, existing, branchId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "            Else" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "                details = details & ""Missing from "" & branchId & "". """ & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "            details = details & misplaced" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "            If Len(details) > 0 Then issues.Add key & "": "" & details" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "    Next record" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "    If IsMissing(conflicts) And issues.count > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        details = ""Weekly conflicts: "" & issues.count & "".""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        For Each issue In issues" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "            details = details & vbCrLf & CStr(issue)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "            If Len(details) > 800 Then Exit For" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        Next issue" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "        FlowError details" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "    If Not IsMissing(conflicts) Then Set conflicts = issues" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows1 = PartLedgerTransferFlows1 & "    Set PlanWeeklyImport = AuditOperationPlan(PlanNewRecords(pending), ""WeeklyImport"")" & vbCrLf
End Function

    PartLedgerTransferFlows2 = PartLedgerTransferFlows2 & "    If LedgerRecordKey(masterBefore, fields) <> LedgerRecordKey(branchBefore, fields) Then FlowError ""Snapshot keys differ.""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows2 = PartLedgerTransferFlows2 & "    RejectHandover masterBefore" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows2 = PartLedgerTransferFlows2 & "    RejectHandover branchBefore" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows2 = PartLedgerTransferFlows2 & "    Set masterPatch = NewLedgerRecord(): Set branchPatch = NewLedgerRecord()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows2 = PartLedgerTransferFlows2 & "    For Each fieldId In changes.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows2 = PartLedgerTransferFlows2 & "        If Not fields.Exists(fieldId) Then FlowError ""Unknown field: "" & CStr(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows2 = PartLedgerTransferFlows2 & "        Set Field = fields(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows2 = PartLedgerTransferFlows2 & "        If Not LedgerValuesEqual(masterBefore(fieldId), changes(fieldId)) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows2 = PartLedgerTransferFlows2 & "            If Field(""Editable"") <> ChrW(&H25CB) Then FlowError ""Field is not editable: "" & CStr(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows2 = PartLedgerTransferFlows2 & "            If CStr(fieldId) = ""OfficeName"" Or Field(""Rank"") > 0 Then FlowError ""Office or key change requires a separate operation.""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows2 = PartLedgerTransferFlows2 & "            masterPatch.Add CStr(fieldId), changes(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows2 = PartLedgerTransferFlows2 & "            If Field(""Branch"") = ChrW(&H25CB) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows2 = PartLedgerTransferFlows2 & "                If Not LedgerValuesEqual(masterBefore(fieldId), branchBefore(fieldId)) Then FlowError ""Master and branch differ before edit: "" & CStr(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows2 = PartLedgerTransferFlows2 & "                branchPatch.Add CStr(fieldId), changes(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows2 = PartLedgerTransferFlows2 & "            End If" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartLedgerTransferFlows3() As String
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    Next fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    If masterPatch.count > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "        instructions.Add NewLedgerInstruction(masterId, masterBefore, masterPatch)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "        If branchPatch.count > 0 Then instructions.Add NewLedgerInstruction(branchId, branchBefore, branchPatch)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    Set PlanIndividualUpdate = AuditOperationPlan(instructions, ""IndividualUpdate"")" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "' Branches are authoritative ONLY for explicitly approved J-column fields." & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "' Missing/ambiguous master records and cross-branch duplicate keys stop the entire plan." & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "Public Function PlanBranchSynchronization(Optional ByVal batch As LedgerReadBatch) As Collection" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    Dim instructions As New Collection, fields As Object, Addresses As Object, masterIndex As Object, branchSeen As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    Dim masterRecords As Collection, branchRecords As Collection, branchRecord As Object, masterRecord As Object, definition As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    Dim patch As Object, Field As Object, fileId As Variant, fieldId As Variant, key As String, masterId As String, syncCount As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    If batch Is Nothing Then Set batch = New LedgerReadBatch" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    Set fields = batch.fields: Set Addresses = batch.Addresses" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    ValidateFlags fields, ""Branch"": ValidateFlags fields, ""Sync""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    For Each fieldId In fields.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "        Set Field = fields(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "        If Field(""Sync"") = ChrW(&H25CB) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "            If Field(""Branch"") <> ChrW(&H25CB) Or Field(""Rank"") > 0 Or CStr(fieldId) = ""OfficeName"" Then FlowError ""Invalid sync field: "" & CStr(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "            syncCount = syncCount + 1" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    Next fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    If syncCount = 0 Then FlowError ""No synchronization fields approved.""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    masterId = MasterFileId(Addresses)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    Set masterRecords = batch.Records(masterId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    Set masterIndex = NewLedgerRecord(): Set branchSeen = NewLedgerRecord()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    For Each masterRecord In masterRecords" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "        key = LedgerRecordKey(masterRecord, fields)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "        masterIndex.Add key, masterRecord" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    Next masterRecord" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    For Each fileId In Addresses.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "        Set definition = Addresses(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "        If definition(""Role"") = ""Branch"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "            Set branchRecords = batch.Records(CStr(fileId))" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "            For Each branchRecord In branchRecords" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "                If RequiredOffice(branchRecord) <> CStr(definition(""Office"")) Then FlowError ""Wrong office in branch: "" & CStr(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "                key = LedgerRecordKey(branchRecord, fields)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "                If branchSeen.Exists(key) Then FlowError ""Same key occurs in multiple branches.""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "                branchSeen.Add key, True" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "                If Not masterIndex.Exists(key) Then FlowError ""Branch record missing from master: "" & CStr(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "                Set masterRecord = masterIndex(key)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "                If RequiredOffice(masterRecord) <> RequiredOffice(branchRecord) Then FlowError ""Master office differs from branch.""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "                Set patch = NewLedgerRecord()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "                For Each fieldId In fields.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "                    Set Field = fields(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "                    If Field(""Sync"") = ChrW(&H25CB) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "                        If Not LedgerValuesEqual(masterRecord(fieldId), branchRecord(fieldId)) Then patch.Add CStr(fieldId), branchRecord(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "                    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "                Next fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "                If patch.count > 0 Then instructions.Add NewLedgerInstruction(masterId, masterRecord, patch)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "            Next branchRecord" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    Next fileId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    Set PlanBranchSynchronization = AuditOperationPlan(instructions, ""BranchSynchronization"")" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "Private Function MasterFileId(ByVal Addresses As Object) As String" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    Dim fileId As Variant, definition As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    For Each fileId In Addresses.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "        Set definition = Addresses(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "        If definition(""Role"") = ""Master"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "            If Len(MasterFileId) > 0 Then FlowError ""Multiple master files.""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "            MasterFileId = CStr(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    Next fileId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "    If Len(MasterFileId) = 0 Then FlowError ""No master file.""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows3 = PartLedgerTransferFlows3 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartLedgerTransferFlows4() As String
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "Private Function BranchFileId(ByVal Addresses As Object, ByVal office As String) As String" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    Dim fileId As Variant, definition As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    For Each fileId In Addresses.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "        Set definition = Addresses(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "        If definition(""Role"") = ""Branch"" And CStr(definition(""Office"")) = office Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "            If Len(BranchFileId) > 0 Then FlowError ""Multiple files for office: "" & office" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "            BranchFileId = CStr(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    Next fileId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    If Len(BranchFileId) = 0 Then FlowError ""Unknown office: "" & office" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "Private Function RequiredOffice(ByVal record As Object) As String" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    If Not record.Exists(""OfficeName"") Then FlowError ""Missing OfficeName.""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    RequiredOffice = CStr(record(""OfficeName""))" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    If Len(RequiredOffice) = 0 Then FlowError ""OfficeName is empty.""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "Private Function SelectFields(ByVal record As Object, ByVal fields As Object, ByVal flagName As String) As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    Dim result As Object, Field As Object, fieldId As Variant" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    Set result = NewLedgerRecord()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    For Each fieldId In record.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "        If Not fields.Exists(fieldId) Then FlowError ""Unknown field: "" & CStr(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "        Set Field = fields(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "        If Field(flagName) = ChrW(&H25CB) Then result.Add CStr(fieldId), record(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    Next fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    Set SelectFields = result" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "Private Sub ValidateFlags(ByVal fields As Object, ByVal flagName As String)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    Dim fieldId As Variant, Field As Object, flag As String" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    For Each fieldId In fields.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "        Set Field = fields(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "        flag = CStr(Field(flagName))" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "        If flag <> ChrW(&H25CB) And flag <> ChrW(&HD7) Then FlowError ""Missing or invalid "" & flagName & "" flag: "" & CStr(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    Next fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "Private Sub RejectHandover(ByVal record As Object)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    If record.Exists(""OfficeHandoverDate"") Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "        If Not IsEmpty(record(""OfficeHandoverDate"")) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "            If Len(CStr(record(""OfficeHandoverDate""))) > 0 Then FlowError ""Transferred case is locked.""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "Private Sub FlowError(ByVal message As String)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 7500, ""LedgerTransferFlows"", message" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "Private Function AuditOperationPlan(ByVal instructions As Collection, ByVal operation As String) As Collection" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    Dim instruction As Object, entries As Collection" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    For Each instruction In instructions" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "        instruction.Add ""Operation"", operation" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    Next instruction" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    If instructions.count = 0 Then AppendOperationAudit OperationActor(), operation, vbNullString, ""NO_CHANGES"", vbNullString, entries" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "    Set AuditOperationPlan = instructions" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerTransferFlows4 = PartLedgerTransferFlows4 & "" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function SourceOperationAudit() As String
    SourceOperationAudit = SourceOperationAudit & PartOperationAudit1()
    SourceOperationAudit = SourceOperationAudit & PartOperationAudit2()
End Function
Private Function PartOperationAudit1() As String
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "Private sessionActor As String" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "Private lastLogError As String" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "Public Sub SetOperationActor(ByVal actor As String)" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    sessionActor = Trim$(actor)" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "Public Function OperationActor() As String" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    If Len(sessionActor) > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "        OperationActor = sessionActor" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "        OperationActor = ReadAuditSetting(""B2"")" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "Public Function OperationLogLastError() As String" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    OperationLogLastError = lastLogError" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "Private Function ReadAuditSetting(ByVal cellAddress As String) As String" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    ReadAuditSetting = Trim$(CStr(ThisWorkbook.Worksheets(""Operations"").range(cellAddress).Value2))" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "' Each entry is Array(file, key, field, encoded before, encoded after)." & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "' The journal remains the only source for resuming a transfer." & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "Public Sub AppendOperationAudit(ByVal actor As String, ByVal operation As String, ByVal transaction As String, _" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "                               ByVal result As String, ByVal message As String, ByVal entries As Collection)" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    Dim path As String, book As Workbook, sheet As Worksheet, candidate As Workbook" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    Dim headers As Variant, data() As Variant, item As Variant, count As Long, firstRow As Long, row As Long, col As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    Dim oldEvents As Boolean, oldSecurity As Long, timestamp As Double, fileName As String, hasAutoSave As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    Dim checkedOut As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    lastLogError = vbNullString" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    path = ReadAuditSetting(""B3"")" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    If Len(path) = 0 Then Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    If Len(actor) = 0 Then actor = ""(unidentified)""" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    oldEvents = Application.EnableEvents: oldSecurity = Application.AutomationSecurity" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    On Error GoTo Failed" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    fileName = Mid$(path, InStrRev(Replace(path, ""\"", ""/""), ""/"") + 1)" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    If LCase$(Right$(fileName, 5)) <> "".xlsx"" Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Audit location must be an .xlsx workbook.""" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    For Each candidate In Application.Workbooks" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "        If StrComp(candidate.name, fileName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Audit workbook already open.""" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    Next candidate" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    Application.EnableEvents = False: Application.AutomationSecurity = 3" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    If LCase$(Left$(path, 8)) = ""https://"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "        If Not Workbooks.CanCheckOut(path) Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Cannot obtain exclusive SharePoint checkout for audit.""" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "        Workbooks.CheckOut path" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "        checkedOut = True" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    Set book = Workbooks.Open(path, UpdateLinks:=0, readOnly:=False, AddToMru:=False, Notify:=False)" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    If book.readOnly Or book.MultiUserEditing Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Audit workbook is not writable exclusively.""" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    hasAutoSave = book.AutoSaveOn" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    On Error GoTo Failed" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    If hasAutoSave Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Disable AutoSave on the audit workbook.""" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    If book.Worksheets.count <> 1 Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Audit workbook must have one sheet.""" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    Set sheet = book.Worksheets(1)" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    headers = Array(""Actor"", ""LocalTimestamp"", ""Operation"", ""Transaction"", ""File"", ""Key"", ""Field"", ""Before"", ""After"", ""Result"", ""Error"")" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    If Application.CountA(sheet.UsedRange) = 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "        For col = 0 To UBound(headers)" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "            sheet.Cells(1, col + 1).Value2 = headers(col)" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "        Next col" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "        For col = 0 To UBound(headers)" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "            If CStr(sheet.Cells(1, col + 1).Value2) <> CStr(headers(col)) Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Unexpected audit workbook schema.""" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "        Next col" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit1 = PartOperationAudit1 & "    firstRow = sheet.Cells(sheet.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row + 1" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartOperationAudit2() As String
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    If Not entries Is Nothing Then count = entries.count" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    If count = 0 Then count = 1" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    If firstRow + count - 1 > sheet.rows.count Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Audit workbook capacity exceeded.""" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    ReDim data(1 To count, 1 To 11)" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    timestamp = CDbl(Now)" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    For row = 1 To count" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "        data(row, 1) = ""'"" & actor: data(row, 2) = timestamp" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "        data(row, 3) = ""'"" & operation: data(row, 4) = ""'"" & transaction" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "        If Not entries Is Nothing Then" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "            If entries.count > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "                item = entries(row)" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "                For col = 0 To 4" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "                    If Len(CStr(item(col))) > 0 Then data(row, col + 5) = ""'"" & CStr(item(col))" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "                Next col" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "        data(row, 10) = ""'"" & result" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "        If Len(message) > 0 Then data(row, 11) = ""'"" & Left$(message, 32760)" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    Next row" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    sheet.Cells(firstRow, 2).Resize(count, 1).NumberFormat = ""yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss""" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    sheet.Cells(firstRow, 1).Resize(count, 11).Value2 = data" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    book.Save" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    If Not book.saved Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Audit workbook save did not complete.""" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    If checkedOut Then" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "        If Not book.CanCheckIn Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Cannot check in the audit workbook.""" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "        book.CheckIn SaveChanges:=True" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "        checkedOut = False" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "        Set book = Nothing" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    GoTo Cleanup" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "Failed:" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    lastLogError = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    Debug.Print ""Operation audit: "" & lastLogError" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "Cleanup:" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    If checkedOut Then" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "        If book Is Nothing Then Set book = Workbooks.Open(path, UpdateLinks:=0, readOnly:=False, AddToMru:=False, Notify:=False)" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "        If Not book Is Nothing Then" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "            If book.CanCheckIn Then book.CheckIn SaveChanges:=False" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    If Not book Is Nothing Then book.Close False" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "    Application.EnableEvents = oldEvents: Application.AutomationSecurity = oldSecurity" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartOperationAudit2 = PartOperationAudit2 & "" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function SourceDailyBackup() As String
    SourceDailyBackup = SourceDailyBackup & PartDailyBackup1()
    SourceDailyBackup = SourceDailyBackup & PartDailyBackup2()
    SourceDailyBackup = SourceDailyBackup & PartDailyBackup3()
    SourceDailyBackup = SourceDailyBackup & PartDailyBackup4()
    SourceDailyBackup = SourceDailyBackup & PartDailyBackup5()
    SourceDailyBackup = SourceDailyBackup & PartDailyBackup6()
    SourceDailyBackup = SourceDailyBackup & PartDailyBackup7()
End Function
Private Function PartDailyBackup1() As String
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "Private Const SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA As String = ""TypedCSV/1""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "Private Type FloatData" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    value As Double" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "End Type" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "Private Type FloatBytes" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    values(0 To 7) As Byte" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "End Type" & vbCrLf
