    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "Private Function SnapshotName(ByVal day As Date) As String" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    SnapshotName = ""B"" & Format$(day, ""yyyymmdd"")" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "Private Function SheetNamed(ByVal book As Workbook, ByVal name As String) As Worksheet" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    Set SheetNamed = book.Worksheets(name)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "Private Function BackupBook(ByVal path As String, ByVal readOnly As Boolean) As Workbook" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    Dim book As Workbook, opened As Workbook, fileName As String, number As Long, message As String" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    Dim oldEvents As Boolean, oldSecurity As Long, hasAutoSave As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    oldEvents = Application.EnableEvents: oldSecurity = Application.AutomationSecurity" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    On Error GoTo Failed" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    fileName = Mid$(path, InStrRev(Replace(path, ""\"", ""/""), ""/"") + 1)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    If LCase$(Right$(fileName, 5)) <> "".xlsx"" Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Expected an .xlsx workbook: "" & path" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    For Each opened In Workbooks" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "        If StrComp(opened.name, fileName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Close the already-open workbook: "" & fileName" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    Next opened" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    Application.EnableEvents = False: Application.AutomationSecurity = 3" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    Set book = Workbooks.Open(path, UpdateLinks:=0, readOnly:=readOnly, AddToMru:=False, Notify:=False)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    If Not readOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "        If book.readOnly Or book.MultiUserEditing Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Workbook is not writable exclusively: "" & path" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "        On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "        hasAutoSave = book.AutoSaveOn" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "        On Error GoTo Failed" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "        If hasAutoSave Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Disable AutoSave before backup or restore: "" & path" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    Application.EnableEvents = oldEvents: Application.AutomationSecurity = oldSecurity" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    Set BackupBook = book" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "Failed:" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    number = Err.number: message = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    If Not book Is Nothing Then book.Close False" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    Application.EnableEvents = oldEvents: Application.AutomationSecurity = oldSecurity" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    Err.Raise number, ""DailyBackup"", message" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "' The workbook is a SharePoint-compatible container for daily typed CSV tables." & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "' Targets is a dictionary of stable identifiers to absolute Excel workbook URLs or paths." & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "Public Function CreateDailyBackup(ByVal archivePath As String, ByVal targets As Object, _" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "                                  ByVal retentionDays As Long, Optional ByVal asOf As Date, Optional ByVal observer As Object) As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    Dim archive As Workbook, snapshot As Worksheet, fileId As Variant, Records As New Collection" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    Dim day As Date, cutoff As Date, data As Variant, index As Long, name As String, nameDate As Date" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    Dim oldAlerts As Boolean, number As Long, message As String, checkedOut As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    oldAlerts = Application.DisplayAlerts" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    On Error GoTo Failed" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    If retentionDays < 1 Or retentionDays > 3650 Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Invalid retention days.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    If targets Is Nothing Then Err.Raise 5, , ""No backup targets.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    If targets.count = 0 Then Err.Raise 5, , ""No backup targets.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    If asOf = 0 Then asOf = Date" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    day = DateValue(asOf)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    cutoff = DateAdd(""d"", 1 - retentionDays, day)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    For Each fileId In targets.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "        If StrComp(Replace(archivePath, ""\"", ""/""), Replace(CStr(targets(fileId)), ""\"", ""/""), vbTextCompare) = 0 Then _" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "            Err.Raise 5, , ""Archive must be separate from all backup targets.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    Next fileId" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup1 = PartDailyBackup1 & "    checkedOut = BeginBackupCheckout(archivePath)" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartDailyBackup2() As String
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    Set archive = BackupBook(archivePath, False)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    ValidateArchive archive" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    Set snapshot = SheetNamed(archive, SnapshotName(day))" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    If snapshot Is Nothing Then" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        For Each fileId In targets.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "            CollectBackupFile Records, CStr(fileId), CStr(targets(fileId)), observer" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        Next fileId" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        If Records.count >= archive.Worksheets(1).rows.count Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Backup sheet capacity exceeded.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        data = SnapshotArray(Records, day)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        Set snapshot = archive.Worksheets.Add(After:=archive.Worksheets(archive.Worksheets.count))" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        snapshot.name = SnapshotName(day)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        snapshot.range(""A1"").Resize(UBound(data, 1), 7).Value2 = data" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        archive.Save" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        archive.Close False: Set archive = Nothing" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        Set archive = BackupBook(archivePath, False)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        Set snapshot = archive.Worksheets(SnapshotName(day))" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        VerifySnapshot snapshot, data" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        CreateDailyBackup = True" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        ValidateSnapshot snapshot" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    ' Retention starts only after a saved, readable snapshot has been verified." & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    Application.DisplayAlerts = False" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    For index = archive.Worksheets.count To 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        name = archive.Worksheets(index).name" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        If name Like ""B########"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "            nameDate = dateSerial(CInt(Mid$(name, 2, 4)), CInt(Mid$(name, 6, 2)), CInt(Right$(name, 2)))" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "            If SnapshotName(nameDate) = name And nameDate < cutoff Then" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "                If CStr(archive.Worksheets(index).range(""A1"").Value2) = SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA Then archive.Worksheets(index).Delete" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    Next index" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    If Not archive.saved Then archive.Save" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    If Not archive.saved Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Backup save did not complete.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    FinishBackupWrite archive, checkedOut" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    Application.DisplayAlerts = oldAlerts" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "Failed:" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    number = Err.number: message = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    CancelBackupWrite archive, archivePath, checkedOut" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    Application.DisplayAlerts = oldAlerts" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    Err.Raise number, ""CreateDailyBackup"", message" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "Private Function BeginBackupCheckout(ByVal path As String) As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    If LCase$(Left$(path, 8)) <> ""https://"" Then Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    If Not Workbooks.CanCheckOut(path) Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Cannot obtain exclusive SharePoint checkout.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    Workbooks.CheckOut path" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    BeginBackupCheckout = True" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "Private Sub FinishBackupWrite(ByRef book As Workbook, ByRef checkedOut As Boolean)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    If checkedOut Then" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        If Not book.CanCheckIn Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Cannot check in the SharePoint workbook.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        book.CheckIn SaveChanges:=True" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        checkedOut = False" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        book.Close False" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    Set book = Nothing" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "Private Sub CancelBackupWrite(ByRef book As Workbook, ByVal path As String, ByVal checkedOut As Boolean)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "    If checkedOut Then" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        If book Is Nothing Then Set book = BackupBook(path, False)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup2 = PartDailyBackup2 & "        If Not book Is Nothing Then" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartDailyBackup3() As String
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "            If book.CanCheckIn Then book.CheckIn SaveChanges:=False" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    If Not book Is Nothing Then book.Close False" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    Set book = Nothing" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "Private Sub ValidateArchive(ByVal book As Workbook)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    Dim sheet As Worksheet" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    For Each sheet In book.Worksheets" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "        If Application.CountA(sheet.UsedRange) > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "            If Not sheet.name Like ""B########"" Or CStr(sheet.range(""A1"").Value2) <> SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA Then _" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "                Err.Raise 5, , ""Backup destination is not a dedicated archive workbook.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    Next sheet" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "Private Sub CollectBackupFile(ByVal Records As Collection, ByVal fileId As String, ByVal path As String, ByVal observer As Object)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    Dim book As Workbook, sheet As Worksheet, cell As range, used As range, values As Variant" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    Dim row As Long, col As Long, value As Variant, kind As String, payload As String" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    Dim number As Long, message As String" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    On Error GoTo Failed" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    Set book = BackupBook(path, True)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    If Not observer Is Nothing Then CallByName observer, ""ReadOpenWorkbook"", VbMethod, fileId, book" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    Records.Add Array(""W"", fileId, """", 0&, 0&, ""B"", IIf(book.Date1904, ""1"", ""0""))" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    For Each sheet In book.Worksheets" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "        Records.Add Array(""S"", fileId, sheet.name, 0&, 0&, """", """")" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "        Set used = sheet.UsedRange" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "        values = used.Value2" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "        For row = 1 To used.rows.count" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "            For col = 1 To used.columns.count" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "                If IsArray(values) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "                    value = values(row, col)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "                Else" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "                    value = values" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "                End If" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "                If Not IsEmpty(value) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "                    Set cell = used.Cells(row, col)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "                    EncodeBackupCell cell, value, kind, payload" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "                    Records.Add Array(""C"", fileId, sheet.name, cell.row, cell.column, kind, payload)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "                End If" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "            Next col" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "        Next row" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    Next sheet" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    book.Close False" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "Failed:" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    number = Err.number: message = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    If Not book Is Nothing Then book.Close False" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    If Not cell Is Nothing Then message = message & "" / cell row "" & row & "", column "" & col" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    Err.Raise number, ""CollectBackupFile"", fileId & "": "" & message" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "Private Sub EncodeBackupCell(ByVal cell As range, ByVal value As Variant, ByRef kind As String, ByRef payload As String)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    Dim spill As Variant" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    If cell.HasFormula Then" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "        On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "        spill = CallByName(cell, ""HasSpill"", VbGet)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "        On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "        If cell.HasArray Or cell.HasSpill Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Array/spill formulas require workbook-level backup: "" & cell.address" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "        kind = ""F"": payload = CStr(cell.Formula2)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "        Select Case VarType(value)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "            Case vbString: kind = ""T"": payload = CStr(value)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "            Case vbBoolean: kind = ""B"": payload = IIf(value, ""1"", ""0"")" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "            Case vbDouble: kind = ""N"": payload = DoubleHex(CDbl(value))" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "            Case vbError: kind = ""E"": payload = CStr(cell.Parent.Evaluate(""ERROR.TYPE("" & cell.address & "")""))" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup3 = PartDailyBackup3 & "            Case Else: Err.Raise 5, , ""Unsupported backup cell type at "" & cell.address" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartDailyBackup4() As String
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "        End Select" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    If Len(payload) > 32766 Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Backup payload exceeds cell capacity at "" & cell.address" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "Private Function DoubleHex(ByVal value As Double) As String" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    Dim number As FloatData, bytes As FloatBytes, index As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    number.value = value" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    LSet bytes = number" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    For index = 0 To 7" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "        DoubleHex = DoubleHex & Right$(""0"" & Hex$(bytes.values(index)), 2)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    Next index" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "Private Function NumberFromHex(ByVal payload As String) As Double" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    Dim number As FloatData, bytes As FloatBytes, index As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    If Len(payload) <> 16 Or payload Like ""*[!0-9A-F]*"" Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Invalid numeric CSV payload.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    For index = 0 To 7" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "        bytes.values(index) = CByte(Val(""&H"" & Mid$(payload, index * 2 + 1, 2)))" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    Next index" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    LSet number = bytes" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    NumberFromHex = number.value" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "Private Function SnapshotArray(ByVal Records As Collection, ByVal day As Date) As Variant" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    Dim data() As Variant, item As Variant, row As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    ReDim data(1 To Records.count + 2, 1 To 7)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    data(1, 1) = SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA: data(1, 2) = CDbl(day): data(1, 3) = Records.count" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    data(2, 1) = ""Record"": data(2, 2) = ""FileId"": data(2, 3) = ""Worksheet""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    data(2, 4) = ""Row"": data(2, 5) = ""Column"": data(2, 6) = ""Type"": data(2, 7) = ""Payload""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    row = 2" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    For Each item In Records" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "        row = row + 1" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "        data(row, 1) = item(0): data(row, 2) = ""S"" & item(1): data(row, 3) = ""S"" & item(2)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "        data(row, 4) = item(3): data(row, 5) = item(4)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "        data(row, 6) = ""S"" & item(5): data(row, 7) = ""S"" & item(6)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    Next item" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    data(1, 4) = SnapshotDigest(data)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    SnapshotArray = data" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "Private Function SnapshotDigest(ByRef data As Variant) As String" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    Dim row As Long, col As Long, i As Long, value As String, hash As Double, mixed As Double" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    Const PRIME As Double = 2147483647#" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    hash = 17#" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    For row = 2 To UBound(data, 1)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "        For col = 1 To 7" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "            value = CStr(data(row, col))" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "            mixed = hash * 65599# + Len(value)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "            hash = mixed - PRIME * Fix(mixed / PRIME)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "            For i = 1 To Len(value)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "                mixed = hash * 65599# + (CLng(AscW(Mid$(value, i, 1))) And 65535)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "                hash = mixed - PRIME * Fix(mixed / PRIME)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "            Next i" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "        Next col" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    Next row" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    SnapshotDigest = ""D"" & Hex$(CLng(hash))" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "Private Sub ValidateSnapshot(ByVal snapshot As Worksheet)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    Dim lastRow As Long, data As Variant" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    With snapshot" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "        If CStr(.range(""A1"").Value2) <> SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Invalid backup schema.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "        If SnapshotName(CDate(.range(""B1"").Value2)) <> .name Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Backup date does not match its name.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "        lastRow = .Cells(.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "        If lastRow < 3 Or lastRow - 2 <> CLng(.range(""C1"").Value2) Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Incomplete backup table.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "        data = .range(""A1:G"" & lastRow).Value2" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "        If CStr(data(1, 4)) <> SnapshotDigest(data) Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Backup content verification failed.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "    End With" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup4 = PartDailyBackup4 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartDailyBackup5() As String
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "Private Sub VerifySnapshot(ByVal snapshot As Worksheet, ByVal expected As Variant)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Dim actual As Variant, row As Long, col As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    ValidateSnapshot snapshot" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    actual = snapshot.range(""A1"").Resize(UBound(expected, 1), 7).Value2" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    For row = 1 To UBound(expected, 1)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "        For col = 1 To 7" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "            If StrComp(CStr(expected(row, col)), CStr(actual(row, col)), vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "                Err.Raise 5, , ""Saved backup differs at row "" & row & "", column "" & col" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "        Next col" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Next row" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "Public Sub RestoreBackupData(ByVal archivePath As String, ByVal day As Date, ByVal fileId As String, ByVal targetPath As String)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Dim archive As Workbook, snapshot As Worksheet, data As Variant, number As Long, message As String" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    On Error GoTo Failed" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Set archive = BackupBook(archivePath, True)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Set snapshot = archive.Worksheets(SnapshotName(day))" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    ValidateSnapshot snapshot" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    data = snapshot.range(""A3:G"" & (CLng(snapshot.range(""C1"").Value2) + 2)).Value2" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    archive.Close False: Set archive = Nothing" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    RestoreBackupRows data, fileId, targetPath" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "Failed:" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    number = Err.number: message = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    If Not archive Is Nothing Then archive.Close False" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Err.Raise number, ""RestoreBackupData"", message" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "Public Sub RestoreBackupCsv(ByVal csvPath As String, ByVal fileId As String, ByVal targetPath As String)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Dim csv As Workbook, existing As Workbook, fileName As String, sheet As Worksheet" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Dim content As Variant, data As Variant, lastRow As Long, number As Long, message As String" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    On Error GoTo Failed" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    fileName = Mid$(csvPath, InStrRev(Replace(csvPath, ""\"", ""/""), ""/"") + 1)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    If LCase$(Right$(fileName, 4)) <> "".csv"" Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Expected a .csv backup.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    For Each existing In Workbooks" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "        If StrComp(existing.name, fileName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Close the already-open CSV.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Next existing" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Workbooks.OpenText fileName:=csvPath, Origin:=65001, DataType:=xlDelimited, Comma:=True, _" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "                       TextQualifier:=xlTextQualifierDoubleQuote, _" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "                       FieldInfo:=Array(Array(1, xlTextFormat), Array(2, xlTextFormat), Array(3, xlTextFormat), _" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "                                        Array(4, xlTextFormat), Array(5, xlTextFormat), Array(6, xlTextFormat), Array(7, xlTextFormat))" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Set csv = Workbooks(fileName)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Set sheet = csv.Worksheets(1)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    lastRow = sheet.Cells(sheet.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    If lastRow < 3 Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Incomplete CSV.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    content = sheet.range(""A1:G"" & lastRow).Value2" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    If CStr(content(1, 1)) <> SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA Or CLng(content(1, 3)) <> lastRow - 2 Then _" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "        Err.Raise 5, , ""CSV schema or record count mismatch.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    If CStr(content(1, 4)) <> SnapshotDigest(content) Then Err.Raise 5, , ""CSV content verification failed.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    data = sheet.range(""A3:G"" & lastRow).Value2" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    csv.Close False: Set csv = Nothing" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    RestoreBackupRows data, fileId, targetPath" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "Failed:" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    number = Err.number: message = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    If Not csv Is Nothing Then csv.Close False" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Err.Raise number, ""RestoreBackupCsv"", message" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "Private Sub RestoreBackupRows(ByRef data As Variant, ByVal fileId As String, ByVal targetPath As String)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Dim target As Workbook, sheet As Worksheet, name As Variant" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Dim row As Long, sheets As Object, found As Boolean, checkedOut As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    Dim number As Long, message As String" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    On Error GoTo Failed" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup5 = PartDailyBackup5 & "    checkedOut = BeginBackupCheckout(targetPath)" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartDailyBackup6() As String
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    Set target = BackupBook(targetPath, False)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    Set sheets = CreateObject(""Scripting.Dictionary"")" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    For row = 1 To UBound(data, 1)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "        If data(row, 2) = ""S"" & fileId Then" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "            If data(row, 1) = ""W"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                If found Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Duplicate workbook manifest.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                found = True" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                If CBool(Val(Mid$(data(row, 7), 2))) <> target.Date1904 Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Date system mismatch.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "            ElseIf data(row, 1) = ""S"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                name = Mid$(data(row, 3), 2)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                Set sheet = target.Worksheets(CStr(name))" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                If sheet.ProtectContents Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Protected restore worksheet: "" & name" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                sheets.Add name, sheet" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    Next row" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    If Not found Or sheets.count <> target.Worksheets.count Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Restore target workbook layout differs.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    For Each name In sheets.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "        Set sheet = sheets(name)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "        sheet.UsedRange.ClearContents" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    Next name" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    ApplyBackupData data, fileId, sheets, False" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    ApplyBackupData data, fileId, sheets, True" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    target.Save" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    If Not target.saved Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Restore save did not complete.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    FinishBackupWrite target, checkedOut" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    Set target = BackupBook(targetPath, True)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    Set sheets = CreateObject(""Scripting.Dictionary"")" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    For Each sheet In target.Worksheets" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "        sheets.Add sheet.name, sheet" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    Next sheet" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    ApplyBackupData data, fileId, sheets, True" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    target.Close False" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "Failed:" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    number = Err.number: message = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    CancelBackupWrite target, targetPath, checkedOut" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    Err.Raise number, ""RestoreBackupData"", message" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "Private Sub ApplyBackupData(ByVal data As Variant, ByVal fileId As String, ByVal sheets As Object, ByVal verifyOnly As Boolean)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    Dim row As Long, sheet As Worksheet, cell As range, kind As String, payload As String, actualKind As String, actualPayload As String" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    Dim codes As Variant" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    codes = Array(0, xlErrNull, xlErrDiv0, xlErrValue, xlErrRef, xlErrName, xlErrNum, xlErrNA, xlErrGettingData)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    For row = 1 To UBound(data, 1)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "        If data(row, 1) = ""C"" And data(row, 2) = ""S"" & fileId Then" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "            Set sheet = sheets(Mid$(data(row, 3), 2))" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "            Set cell = sheet.Cells(CLng(data(row, 4)), CLng(data(row, 5)))" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "            kind = Mid$(data(row, 6), 2): payload = Mid$(data(row, 7), 2)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "            If verifyOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                EncodeBackupCell cell, cell.Value2, actualKind, actualPayload" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                If actualKind <> kind Or StrComp(actualPayload, payload, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                    Err.Raise 5, , ""Restored value differs at "" & sheet.name & ""!"" & cell.address" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "            Else" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                Select Case kind" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                    Case ""F"": cell.Formula2 = payload" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                    Case ""T"": cell.Value2 = ""'"" & payload" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                    Case ""B""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                        If payload <> ""0"" And payload <> ""1"" Then Err.Raise 5, , ""Invalid Boolean CSV payload.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                        cell.Value2 = (payload = ""1"")" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                    Case ""N"": cell.Value2 = NumberFromHex(payload)" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                    Case ""E"": cell.Value2 = CVErr(codes(CLng(payload)))" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                    Case Else: Err.Raise 5, , ""Unknown typed CSV record.""" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "                End Select" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "    Next row" & vbCrLf
    PartDailyBackup6 = PartDailyBackup6 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
End Function

    PartDailyBackup7 = PartDailyBackup7 & "    If Not archive Is Nothing Then archive.Close False" & vbCrLf

Private Function PartLedgerControllerForm3() As String
    PartLedgerControllerForm3 = PartLedgerControllerForm3 & "    For Each fieldId In fields.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm3 = PartLedgerControllerForm3 & "        If Not editors.Exists(fieldId) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm3 = PartLedgerControllerForm3 & "            AddEditor CStr(fieldId), 9 + 376 * (offset Mod 2), base + 96 + 24 * (offset \ 2), False" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm3 = PartLedgerControllerForm3 & "            offset = offset + 1" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm3 = PartLedgerControllerForm3 & "        End If" & vbCrLf

Private Function PartLedgerControllerForm6() As String
    PartLedgerControllerForm6 = PartLedgerControllerForm6 & "        list.Clear" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm6 = PartLedgerControllerForm6 & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm6 = PartLedgerControllerForm6 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm6 = PartLedgerControllerForm6 & "    If isSearch Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm6 = PartLedgerControllerForm6 & "        ReDim data(0 To window.ItemCount - 1, 0 To 4)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm6 = PartLedgerControllerForm6 & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm6 = PartLedgerControllerForm6 & "        ReDim data(0 To window.ItemCount - 1, 0 To 3)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm6 = PartLedgerControllerForm6 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm6 = PartLedgerControllerForm6 & "    For index = window.FirstIndex To window.LastIndex" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm6 = PartLedgerControllerForm6 & "        Set record = rows(index)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm6 = PartLedgerControllerForm6 & "        If isSearch Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm6 = PartLedgerControllerForm6 & "            data(row, 0) = record(""OfficeName"")" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm6 = PartLedgerControllerForm6 & "            data(row, 1) = record(""PersonName"")" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm6 = PartLedgerControllerForm6 & "            data(row, 2) = record(""TaxPeriod"")" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm6 = PartLedgerControllerForm6 & "            data(row, 3) = record(""ApplicationType"")" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm6 = PartLedgerControllerForm6 & "            data(row, 4) = record(""ProcessingCategory"")" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm6 = PartLedgerControllerForm6 & "        Else" & vbCrLf

Private Function PartLedgerControllerForm9() As String
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "    If current Is Nothing Then Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "    key = LedgerRecordKey(current, fields)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "    Set rows = hits" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "    If Not rows Is Nothing Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "        For Each record In rows" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "            index = index + 1" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "            If LedgerRecordKey(record, fields) = key Then Exit For" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "        Next record" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "        If index <= rows.count Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "            If index > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "                If LedgerRecordKey(rows(index), fields) = key Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "                    SelectSearchRow index + offset" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "                    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "                End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "    Set rows = ControllerRows()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "    index = 0" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "    For Each record In rows" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "        index = index + 1" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "        If LedgerRecordKey(record, fields) = key Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "            If index + offset >= 1 And index + offset <= rows.count Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "                If LeaveDraft() Then ShowRecord rows(index + offset)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "            Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm9 = PartLedgerControllerForm9 & "        End If" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "Private Function LocalWeeklyIssue(ByVal issue As String) As String" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "    Dim separator As Long, tail As String, item As Object, id As Variant, label As String, pattern As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "    Dim Addresses As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "    separator = InStr(1, issue, "": "", vbBinaryCompare)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "    If separator < 1 Then" & vbCrLf

    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "    tail = Mid$(issue, separator + 2)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "    Set pattern = CreateObject(""VBScript.RegExp"")" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "    pattern.Global = True" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "    For Each id In fields.keys" & vbCrLf
