    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "            tail = pattern.Replace(tail, CStr(item(""Header"")))" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "    Next id" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "    Set Addresses = LoadLedgerAddresses()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "    For Each id In Addresses.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "        If Not CStr(id) Like ""*[!A-Za-z0-9_]*"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "            Set item = Addresses(id)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "            label = CStr(item(""Office""))" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "            If item(""Role"") = ""Master"" Then label = ""全体""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "            pattern.pattern = ""\b"" & CStr(id) & ""\b""" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "            tail = pattern.Replace(tail, label)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "    Next id" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "    tail = Replace(tail, "" differs: "", ""の差異："")" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "    tail = Replace(tail, ""Missing from "", ""未登録："")" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "    tail = Replace(tail, ""Also exists in "", ""別の署に登録済み："")" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "    LocalWeeklyIssue = Left$(issue, separator - 1) & ""："" & tail" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerControllerForm12 = PartLedgerControllerForm12 & "" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function SourceControllerData() As String
    SourceControllerData = SourceControllerData & PartControllerData1()
    SourceControllerData = SourceControllerData & PartControllerData2()
    SourceControllerData = SourceControllerData & PartControllerData3()
    SourceControllerData = SourceControllerData & PartControllerData4()
End Function
Private Function PartControllerData1() As String
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "Private cachedRows As Collection" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "Private cachedFields As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "Private cachedAddresses As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "Private cachedTypes As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "Private masterId As String" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "Private masterByKey As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "Private branchesByOffice As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "Private cacheRequiresReload As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "Public Sub RefreshControllerData(Optional ByVal batch As LedgerReadBatch)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    Dim fields As Object, Addresses As Object, rows As Collection, kinds As Object, definition As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    Dim fileId As Variant, fieldId As Variant, record As Object, currentMaster As String, kind As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    If batch Is Nothing Then Set batch = New LedgerReadBatch" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    Set fields = batch.fields" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    Set Addresses = batch.Addresses" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    For Each fileId In Addresses.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "        Set definition = Addresses(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "        If definition(""Role"") = ""Master"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "            If Len(currentMaster) > 0 Then Err.Raise 5, , ""全体管理簿が複数設定されています。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "            currentMaster = CStr(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    Next fileId" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    If Len(currentMaster) = 0 Then Err.Raise 5, , ""全体管理簿が設定されていません。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    Set rows = batch.Records(currentMaster)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    Set kinds = NewLedgerRecord()" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    For Each fieldId In fields.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "        kinds.Add fieldId, CLng(vbEmpty)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    Next fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    For Each record In rows" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "        For Each fieldId In fields.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "            kind = VarType(record(fieldId))" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "            If kind <> vbEmpty Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "                If kinds(fieldId) = vbEmpty Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "                    kinds(fieldId) = kind" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "                ElseIf kinds(fieldId) <> kind Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "                    kinds(fieldId) = vbVariant" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "                End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "        Next fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    Next record" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    Dim nextMaster As Object, nextBranches As Object, branchRows As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    Set nextMaster = IndexControllerRows(rows, fields)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    Set nextBranches = NewLedgerRecord()" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    For Each fileId In Addresses.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "        Set definition = Addresses(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "        If definition(""Role"") = ""Branch"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "            Set branchRows = TryReadBranchIndex(CStr(fileId), fields, batch)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "            nextBranches.Add CStr(definition(""Office"")), branchRows" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    Next fileId" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    Set masterByKey = nextMaster" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    Set branchesByOffice = nextBranches" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    Set cachedFields = fields: Set cachedAddresses = Addresses" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    Set cachedTypes = kinds: Set cachedRows = rows: masterId = currentMaster" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    cacheRequiresReload = False" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "Public Sub InvalidateControllerData()" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    cacheRequiresReload = True" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "Public Sub EnsureControllerData()" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    If cacheRequiresReload Then Err.Raise 5, , ""保存後の再取得が完了していません。「再読込」を実行してください。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "    If cachedRows Is Nothing Then RefreshControllerData" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData1 = PartControllerData1 & "Public Function ControllerRows() As Collection" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartControllerData2() As String
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    EnsureControllerData" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Set ControllerRows = cachedRows" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "Public Function ControllerFields() As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    EnsureControllerData" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Set ControllerFields = cachedFields" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "Public Function ControllerOfficeNames() As Collection" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Dim names As New Collection, id As Variant, item As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    EnsureControllerData" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    For Each id In cachedAddresses.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "        Set item = cachedAddresses(id)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "        If item(""Role"") = ""Branch"" Then names.Add CStr(item(""Office""))" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Next id" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Set ControllerOfficeNames = names" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "Private Function IndexControllerRows(ByVal rows As Collection, ByVal fields As Object) As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Dim index As Object, record As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Set index = NewLedgerRecord()" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    For Each record In rows" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "        index.Add LedgerRecordKey(record, fields), record" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Next record" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Set IndexControllerRows = index" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "Private Function TryReadBranchIndex(ByVal fileId As String, ByVal fields As Object, ByVal batch As LedgerReadBatch) As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    On Error GoTo Unavailable" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Set TryReadBranchIndex = IndexControllerRows(batch.Records(fileId), fields)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "Unavailable:" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Debug.Print ""Branch cache "" & fileId & "": "" & Err.number & "" "" & Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "Private Function SnapshotControllerRecord(ByVal record As Object) As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Dim snapshot As Object, fieldId As Variant" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Set snapshot = NewLedgerRecord()" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    For Each fieldId In record.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "        snapshot.Add fieldId, record(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Next fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Set SnapshotControllerRecord = snapshot" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "Public Function ControllerMasterRecord(ByVal identity As Object) As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Dim key As String" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    EnsureControllerData" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    key = LedgerRecordKey(identity, cachedFields)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    If Not masterByKey.Exists(key) Then Err.Raise 5, , ""全体管理簿の取得済みデータに対象事案がありません。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Set ControllerMasterRecord = SnapshotControllerRecord(masterByKey(key))" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "Public Function ControllerBranchRecord(ByVal identity As Object) As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Dim office As String, key As String, index As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    EnsureControllerData" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    office = CStr(identity(""OfficeName""))" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    If Not branchesByOffice.Exists(office) Then Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Set index = branchesByOffice(office)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    If index Is Nothing Then Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    key = LedgerRecordKey(identity, cachedFields)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    If index.Exists(key) Then Set ControllerBranchRecord = SnapshotControllerRecord(index(key))" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "Public Function ReadLatestControllerMaster(ByVal identity As Object) As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    EnsureControllerData" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "    Set ReadLatestControllerMaster = ReadLedgerRecord(masterId, identity)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData2 = PartControllerData2 & "Public Function ReadLatestControllerBranch(ByVal identity As Object) As Object" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartControllerData3() As String
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    Dim id As Variant, definition As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    EnsureControllerData" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    For Each id In cachedAddresses.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "        Set definition = cachedAddresses(id)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "        If definition(""Role"") = ""Branch"" And CStr(definition(""Office"")) = CStr(identity(""OfficeName"")) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "            Set ReadLatestControllerBranch = ReadLedgerRecord(CStr(id), identity)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "            Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    Next id" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    Err.Raise 5, , ""対象署の管理簿が設定されていません。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "Public Function ControllerSetting(ByVal cellAddress As String) As String" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    ControllerSetting = Trim$(CStr(ThisWorkbook.Worksheets(""Operations"").range(cellAddress).Value2))" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "Public Function ControllerFieldType(ByVal fieldId As String) As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    EnsureControllerData" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    ControllerFieldType = cachedTypes(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "Public Sub OpenControllerSource(ByVal identity As Object)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    Dim item As Object, book As Workbook, current As Workbook, sheet As Worksheet, wanted As Object, keyFields As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    Dim Field As Object, fieldId As Variant, key As String, row As Long, column As Long, lastRow As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    Dim openedHere As Boolean, number As Long, message As String, match As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    EnsureControllerData" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    Set item = cachedAddresses(masterId)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    On Error GoTo Failed" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    For Each current In Application.Workbooks" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "        If StrComp(current.FullName, CStr(item(""Address"")), vbTextCompare) = 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "            Set book = current" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "            Exit For" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    Next current" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    If book Is Nothing Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "        Set book = Application.Workbooks.Open(CStr(item(""Address"")), UpdateLinks:=0, readOnly:=True, AddToMru:=False)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "        openedHere = True" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    Set sheet = book.Worksheets(CStr(item(""Sheet"")))" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    Set keyFields = NewLedgerRecord()" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    For Each fieldId In cachedFields.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "        Set Field = cachedFields(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "        If Field(""Rank"") > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "            column = CLng(Field(""Column""))" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "            If CStr(sheet.Cells(CLng(item(""HeaderRow"")), column).Value2) <> CStr(Field(""Header"")) Then Err.Raise 5, , ""照合キーの見出しが一致しません。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "            keyFields.Add fieldId, column" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "            row = sheet.Cells(sheet.rows.count, column).End(xlUp).row" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "            If row > lastRow Then lastRow = row" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    Next fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    For row = CLng(item(""FirstRow"")) To lastRow" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "        Set wanted = NewLedgerRecord()" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "        For Each fieldId In keyFields.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "            wanted.Add fieldId, sheet.Cells(row, keyFields(fieldId)).Value2" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "        Next fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "        If ControllerKeysMatch(wanted, identity, keyFields) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "            If match > 0 Then Err.Raise 5, , ""照合キーに一致する行が複数あります。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "            match = row" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    Next row" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    If match = 0 Then Err.Raise 5, , ""元の管理簿に対象行がありません。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    book.Activate" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    Application.GoTo sheet.Cells(match, 1), True" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "Failed:" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    number = Err.number: message = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    If openedHere Then book.Close False" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData3 = PartControllerData3 & "    Err.Raise number, ""OpenControllerSource"", message" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartControllerData4() As String
    PartControllerData4 = PartControllerData4 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData4 = PartControllerData4 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData4 = PartControllerData4 & "Private Function ControllerKeysMatch(ByVal record As Object, ByVal identity As Object, ByVal keyFields As Object) As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData4 = PartControllerData4 & "    Dim fieldId As Variant" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData4 = PartControllerData4 & "    For Each fieldId In keyFields.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData4 = PartControllerData4 & "        If Not LedgerValuesEqual(record(fieldId), identity(fieldId)) Then Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData4 = PartControllerData4 & "    Next fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData4 = PartControllerData4 & "    ControllerKeysMatch = True" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerData4 = PartControllerData4 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function SourceControllerActions() As String
    SourceControllerActions = SourceControllerActions & PartControllerActions1()
    SourceControllerActions = SourceControllerActions & PartControllerActions2()
    SourceControllerActions = SourceControllerActions & PartControllerActions3()
End Function
Private Function PartControllerActions1() As String
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "Public Function ControllerFind(ByVal namePart As String, ByVal office As String, ByVal pendingOnly As Boolean) As Collection" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    Dim rows As Collection, result As New Collection, record As Object, fields As Object, completionField As String" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    Set rows = ControllerRows()" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    If pendingOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "        completionField = ControllerSetting(""B7"")" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "        Set fields = ControllerFields()" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "        If Len(completionField) = 0 Or Not fields.Exists(completionField) Then Err.Raise 5, , ""未済判定の完了日項目をOperationsで指定してください。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    namePart = Trim$(namePart)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    For Each record In rows" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "        If Len(office) = 0 Or CStr(record(""OfficeName"")) = office Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "            If Len(namePart) = 0 Or InStr(1, CStr(record(""PersonName"")), namePart, vbTextCompare) > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "                If Not pendingOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "                    result.Add record" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "                ElseIf Len(CStr(record(completionField))) = 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "                    result.Add record" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "                End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    Next record" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    Set ControllerFind = result" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "Public Function ControllerRelated(ByVal current As Object) As Collection" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    Dim result As New Collection, rows As Collection, record As Object, common As String" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    common = CStr(current(""CommonNumber""))" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    If Len(common) = 13 And Not common Like ""*[!0-9]*"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "        Set rows = ControllerRows()" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "        For Each record In rows" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "            If CStr(record(""CommonNumber"")) = common Then result.Add record" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "        Next record" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    Set ControllerRelated = result" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "Public Function ControllerLocked(ByVal record As Object) As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    If record Is Nothing Then Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    ControllerLocked = Len(CStr(record(""OfficeHandoverDate""))) > 0" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "Public Function ControllerDisplay(ByVal value As Variant, ByVal definition As Object) As String" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    If IsEmpty(value) Then Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    If definition(""Conversion"") = ""Date"" And VarType(value) = vbDouble Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "        If value >= 1 And value <= 2958465# Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "            ControllerDisplay = Format$(CDate(value), ""yyyy/mm/dd"")" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "            Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    ControllerDisplay = CStr(value)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "Public Function ControllerInput(ByVal fieldId As String, ByVal entered As String, ByVal oldValue As Variant, ByVal Field As Object) As Variant" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    Dim kind As Long, day As Date" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    If entered = ControllerDisplay(oldValue, Field) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "        ControllerInput = oldValue" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "        Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    If Len(entered) = 0 Then Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    kind = VarType(oldValue)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    If kind = vbEmpty Then kind = ControllerFieldType(fieldId)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "    If Field(""Conversion"") = ""Date"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "        day = ControllerDate(entered)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "        If kind = vbString Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "            If Len(CStr(oldValue)) = 8 And Not CStr(oldValue) Like ""*[!0-9]*"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "                ControllerInput = Format$(day, ""yyyymmdd"")" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "            Else" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "                ControllerInput = Format$(day, ""yyyy/mm/dd"")" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions1 = PartControllerActions1 & "            End If" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartControllerActions2() As String
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        ElseIf VarType(oldValue) = vbDouble Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "            If oldValue >= 10000000# And oldValue <= 99999999# Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "                ControllerInput = CDbl(Format$(day, ""yyyymmdd""))" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "            Else" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "                ControllerInput = CDbl(day)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        Else" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "            ControllerInput = CDbl(day)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    ElseIf kind = vbDouble Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        If Not IsNumeric(entered) Then Err.Raise 5, , CStr(Field(""Header"")) & ""には数値を入力してください。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        ControllerInput = CDbl(entered)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    ElseIf kind = vbBoolean Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        If LCase$(entered) <> ""true"" And LCase$(entered) <> ""false"" Then Err.Raise 5, , CStr(Field(""Header"")) & ""には True または False を入力してください。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        ControllerInput = (LCase$(entered) = ""true"")" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        ControllerInput = entered" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "Public Function ControllerDate(ByVal value As String) As Date" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    Dim day As Date" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    If Len(value) = 8 And Not value Like ""*[!0-9]*"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        day = dateSerial(CInt(Left$(value, 4)), CInt(Mid$(value, 5, 2)), CInt(Right$(value, 2)))" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        If Format$(day, ""yyyymmdd"") <> value Then Err.Raise 5, , ""日付が正しくありません。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        If Not IsDate(value) Then Err.Raise 5, , ""日付は yyyy/mm/dd で入力してください。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        day = DateValue(value)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    ControllerDate = day" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "Public Function ControllerPlanSave(ByVal master As Object, ByVal branch As Object, ByVal changes As Object, ByVal isNew As Boolean) As Collection" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    Dim key As String" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    If isNew Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        key = LedgerRecordKey(changes, ControllerFields())" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        Set ControllerPlanSave = PlanIndividualRegistration(changes)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        If ControllerLocked(master) Then Err.Raise 5, , ""署引継日が入力済みのため更新できません。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        If branch Is Nothing Then Err.Raise 5, , ""対象署の編集開始時データを取得できなかったため更新できません。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        Set ControllerPlanSave = PlanIndividualUpdate(master, branch, changes)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "Public Function ControllerExecute(ByVal instructions As Collection, ByVal journal As String, ByVal hasExclusiveAccess As Boolean) As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    If instructions.count = 0 Or Len(journal) = 0 Then Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    ExecuteLedgerTransfer instructions, journal, hasExclusiveAccess" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    ControllerExecute = True" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "Public Function ControllerNewJournal(ByVal folder As String) As String" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    Dim path As String, book As Workbook, separator As String, stamp As String, number As Long, message As String" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    If Len(folder) = 0 Then Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    separator = ""\""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    If LCase$(Left$(folder, 8)) = ""https://"" Then separator = ""/""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    If Right$(folder, 1) <> separator Then folder = folder & separator" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    Randomize" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    stamp = Format$(Now, ""yyyymmdd_hhnnss"") & ""_"" & Format$(Int(Rnd() * 1000000), ""000000"")" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    path = folder & ""LedgerJournal_"" & stamp & "".xlsx""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    On Error GoTo Failed" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    If separator = ""\"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "        If Len(Dir$(path)) > 0 Then Err.Raise 5, , ""復旧ジャーナル名が重複しました。もう一度実行してください。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    Set book = Workbooks.Add(xlWBATWorksheet)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    book.SaveAs fileName:=path, FileFormat:=xlOpenXMLWorkbook, AddToMru:=False" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    book.Close False" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    ControllerNewJournal = path" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "Failed:" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions2 = PartControllerActions2 & "    number = Err.number: message = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function PartControllerActions3() As String
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    If Not book Is Nothing Then book.Close False" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    Err.Raise number, ""ControllerNewJournal"", message" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "Public Sub OpenLedgerController()" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    OpenLedgerControllerWithBatch Nothing" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "Public Sub OpenLedgerControllerWithBatch(ByVal batch As LedgerReadBatch)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    Dim owner As window" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    On Error GoTo Failed" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    If ThisWorkbook.Windows.count = 0 Then Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    Set owner = ThisWorkbook.Windows(1)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    owner.Activate" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    If Not batch Is Nothing Then RefreshControllerData batch" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    LedgerControllerForm.ShowInitial" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "Failed:" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    MsgBox Err.Description, vbExclamation, ""管理簿コントローラー""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "Public Function CloseLedgerController() As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    Dim item As Object, controller As LedgerControllerForm" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    For Each item In VBA.UserForms" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "        If TypeOf item Is LedgerControllerForm Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "            Set controller = item" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "            Exit For" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    Next item" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    If Not controller Is Nothing Then" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "        If Not controller.PermitWorkbookClose() Then Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    If Not controller Is Nothing Then Unload controller" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    CloseLedgerController = True" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "Public Sub ControllerVerifyUpdate(ByVal masterBefore As Object, ByVal branchBefore As Object)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    VerifyControllerSnapshot masterBefore, ReadLatestControllerMaster(masterBefore), ""全体""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    VerifyControllerSnapshot branchBefore, ReadLatestControllerBranch(masterBefore), ""対象署""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "Private Sub VerifyControllerSnapshot(ByVal before As Object, ByVal latest As Object, ByVal fileLabel As String)" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    Dim fieldId As Variant" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    If ControllerLocked(latest) Then Err.Raise 5, , fileLabel & ""の事案に署引継日が入力済みのため更新できません。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    For Each fieldId In before.keys" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "        If Not LedgerValuesEqual(before(fieldId), latest(fieldId)) Then _" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "            Err.Raise 5, , fileLabel & ""の事案が編集開始後に変更されました。変更内容を控えて再読込みしてください。""" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "    Next fieldId" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartControllerActions3 = PartControllerActions3 & "" & vbCrLf
End Function

Private Function PartExtendedListBox12() As String
    PartExtendedListBox12 = PartExtendedListBox12 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox12 = PartExtendedListBox12 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox12 = PartExtendedListBox12 & "Private Sub MeasureViewport(ByVal headingHeight As Single, ByRef Width As Single, ByRef Height As Single, ByRef widths() As Single, ByRef totalWidth As Single, ByRef hasVertical As Boolean, ByRef hasHorizontal As Boolean)" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox12 = PartExtendedListBox12 & "    Dim columnIndex As Long" & vbCrLf

    PartExtendedListBox12 = PartExtendedListBox12 & "" & vbCrLf

    PartExtendedListBox12 = PartExtendedListBox12 & "    Do" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox12 = PartExtendedListBox12 & "        Width = host.InsideWidth" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox12 = PartExtendedListBox12 & "        Height = host.InsideHeight" & vbCrLf

    PartExtendedListBox12 = PartExtendedListBox12 & "        If hasHorizontal Then Height = Height - SCROLL_WIDTH" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox12 = PartExtendedListBox12 & "        Width = MaxSingle(1, Width)" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox12 = PartExtendedListBox12 & "        Height = MaxSingle(1, Height)" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox12 = PartExtendedListBox12 & "        ColumnLayout Width, widths" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox12 = PartExtendedListBox12 & "        totalWidth = 0" & vbCrLf

    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    Dim Field As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    Dim autoCount As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    Dim remaining As Single" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    Dim automatic As Single" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    ReDim widths(0 To columnCountValue - 1)" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    fields = Split(customWidths & "";"", "";"")" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    remaining = Width" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    For Field = 0 To columnCountValue - 1" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "        widths(Field) = -1" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "        If Field <= UBound(fields) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "            If Len(Trim$(fields(Field))) > 0 Then widths(Field) = ParseWidth(fields(Field))" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "        If widths(Field) < 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "            autoCount = autoCount + 1" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "        Else" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "            remaining = remaining - widths(Field)" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    Next Field" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    If autoCount = 0 Then Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    automatic = MaxSingle(36, remaining / autoCount)" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    For Field = 0 To columnCountValue - 1" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "        If widths(Field) < 0 Then widths(Field) = automatic" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    Next Field" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "Private Function ParseWidth(ByVal specification As String) As Single" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    Dim magnitude As String" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    Dim factor As Single" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    specification = LCase$(Trim$(specification))" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    factor = 1" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    If Right$(specification, 2) = ""pt"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "        specification = Left$(specification, Len(specification) - 2)" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    ElseIf Right$(specification, 2) = ""cm"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "        factor = 72 / 2.54" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "        specification = Left$(specification, Len(specification) - 2)" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    ElseIf Right$(specification, 2) = ""in"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "        factor = 72" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "        specification = Left$(specification, Len(specification) - 2)" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    magnitude = Trim$(specification)" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    If Not IsNumeric(magnitude) Then Err.Raise 380, ""ExtendedListBox.ColumnWidths""" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    ParseWidth = CSng(magnitude) * factor" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    If ParseWidth < 0 Then Err.Raise 380, ""ExtendedListBox.ColumnWidths""" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "Private Sub ConfigureScroll(ByVal bar As MSForms.ScrollBar, ByVal position As Long, ByVal maximum As Long, ByVal Page As Long)" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    Dim range As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    range = MinLong(maximum, SCROLL_LIMIT)" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    If bar.value > range Then bar.value = range" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    bar.Max = range" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    bar.SmallChange = 1" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    If maximum = 0 Then Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    bar.LargeChange = MinLong(CLng(MaxSingle(1, range \ 2)), CLng(MaxSingle(1, CDbl(Page) * range / maximum)))" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    bar.value = CLng(CDbl(position) * range / maximum)" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "Private Sub Vertical_Change()" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    If isRendering Or vertical.Max = 0 Then Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    firstRow = CLng(CDbl(vertical.value) * lastTopRow / vertical.Max)" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "    Render" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartExtendedListBox13 = PartExtendedListBox13 & "" & vbCrLf

Private Function PartfrmCalendar3() As String
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "    Next index" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "Private Function NormalizeHolidayDates(ByVal holidayDates As Variant) As Collection" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "    Dim result As New Collection" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "    Dim item As Variant" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "    Dim index As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "    If IsMissing(holidayDates) Or IsEmpty(holidayDates) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "        Set NormalizeHolidayDates = result" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "        Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "    If IsObject(holidayDates) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "        If TypeName(holidayDates) = ""Collection"" Then" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "            For Each item In holidayDates" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "                AddHolidayDate result, item" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "            Next item" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "    ElseIf IsArray(holidayDates) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "        For index = LBound(holidayDates) To UBound(holidayDates)" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "            AddHolidayDate result, holidayDates(index)" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "        Next index" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "        AddHolidayDate result, holidayDates" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "    Set NormalizeHolidayDates = result" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "Private Sub AddHolidayDate(ByRef target As Collection, ByVal value As Variant)" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "    If Not IsDate(value) Then Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "    target.Add True, CStr(CLng(DateValue(CDate(value))))" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "    On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartfrmCalendar3 = PartfrmCalendar3 & "" & vbCrLf

Private Function SourceCollectionPaging() As String
    SourceCollectionPaging = SourceCollectionPaging & PartCollectionPaging1()
End Function
Private Function PartCollectionPaging1() As String
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "Public Type PageWindow" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "    Page As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "    PageCount As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "    FirstIndex As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "    LastIndex As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "    ItemCount As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "End Type" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "Public Function GetPageWindow(ByVal total As Long, ByVal requested As Long, ByVal pageSize As Long) As PageWindow" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "    Dim result As PageWindow, remaining As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "    If total < 0 Or pageSize < 1 Then Err.Raise 5, ""CollectionPaging"", ""Invalid total or page size.""" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "    If total > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "        result.PageCount = (total - 1) \ pageSize + 1" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "        result.Page = requested" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "        If result.Page < 1 Then result.Page = 1" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "        If result.Page > result.PageCount Then result.Page = result.PageCount" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "        result.FirstIndex = (result.Page - 1) * pageSize + 1" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "        remaining = total - result.FirstIndex + 1" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "        result.ItemCount = pageSize" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "        If remaining < pageSize Then result.ItemCount = remaining" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "        result.LastIndex = result.FirstIndex + (result.ItemCount - 1)" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "    GetPageWindow = result" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "Public Function PageForItem(ByVal itemIndex As Long, ByVal pageSize As Long) As Long" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "    If itemIndex < 1 Or pageSize < 1 Then Err.Raise 5, ""CollectionPaging"", ""Invalid item index or page size.""" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "    PageForItem = (itemIndex - 1) \ pageSize + 1" & vbCrLf
    PartCollectionPaging1 = PartCollectionPaging1 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
End Function
Private Function SourceLedgerReadBatch() As String
    SourceLedgerReadBatch = SourceLedgerReadBatch & PartLedgerReadBatch1()
End Function
Private Function PartLedgerReadBatch1() As String
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "' A single startup or reload owns this batch. Only successfully validated reads are retained." & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "Private fieldDefinitions As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "Private addressDefinitions As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "Private recordsByFile As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "Private Sub Class_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "    Set recordsByFile = NewLedgerRecord()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "Public Property Get fields() As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "    If fieldDefinitions Is Nothing Then Set fieldDefinitions = LoadLedgerFields()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "    Set fields = fieldDefinitions" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "End Property" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "Public Property Get Addresses() As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "    If addressDefinitions Is Nothing Then Set addressDefinitions = LoadLedgerAddresses()" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "    Set Addresses = addressDefinitions" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "End Property" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "Public Function Records(ByVal fileId As String) As Collection" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "    If Not recordsByFile.Exists(fileId) Then recordsByFile.Add fileId, ReadLedgerRecords(fileId, Addresses, fields)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "    Set Records = recordsByFile(fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "Public Sub ReadOpenWorkbook(ByVal fileId As String, ByVal book As Workbook)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "    Dim definitions As Object" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "    ' The backup can still succeed when a ledger cannot be used by the controller." & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "    ' Cache loading reports or handles the failed read through its usual path." & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "    On Error GoTo Unavailable" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "    Set definitions = Addresses" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "    If Not recordsByFile.Exists(fileId) Then" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "        recordsByFile.Add fileId, ReadOpenLedgerRecords(book, definitions(fileId), fields, fileId)" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "Unavailable:" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "    Debug.Print ""Startup ledger preload "" & fileId & "": "" & Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    PartLedgerReadBatch1 = PartLedgerReadBatch1 & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
End Function
